let body = "";
let count = 0;

req.on("data", (chunk) => {
    count++;

    console.log("Chunk number:", count);
    console.log("Chunk size:", chunk.length);

    body += chunk;
});

req.on("end", () => {
    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

req.on("end", () => {
    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

    count++;

    console.log("Chunk number:", count);
    console.log("Chunk size:", chunk.length);

    body += chunk;
});

req.on("end", () => {
    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");

    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");

req.on("data", (chunk) => {
    count++;

    console.log("Chunk number:", count);
    console.log("Chunk size:", chunk.length);

    body += chunk;
});

req.on("end", () => {
    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

req.on("end", () => {
    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

req.on("end", () => {
    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

//Delete

else if (req.method == "POST" && req.url == "/deleteuser") {

    let body = "";

    req.on("data", (chunk) => {
        body += chunk.toString();
    });

    req.on("end", () => {

        try {
            let user = JSON.parse(body);

            const allusersdata = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync("./users.json", "utf8"));

            let newusers = allusersdata.filter(ele => ele.id !== user.id);

    
            fs.writeFileSync("./users.json", JSON.stringify(newusers, null, 2));

            res.write("User deleted successfully");
            res.end();

        }
        catch (error) {
            res.write("Error is there");
            res.end();
        }

    });
}
